產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 3 日

1 群益投顧 
市 場 流 傳 一 份 疑 似 台 達 電(2308 TT) 發 給 客 戶 的 通 知 指 出，特 定 批 次 電 源 供 應 器 存 在 潛
在 品 質 問 題 。 此 次 問 題 主 要 來 自 供 應 商 零 組 件 ， 並 非 台 達 電 產 品 設 計 ， 且 台 達 電 擁 有
不 同 電 容 器 供 應 商 及 備 料 ， 預 期 可 透 過 更 換 電 容 器 供 應 來 源 解 決， 影 響 有 限。 台達電
為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商 ， 受 惠 於AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動 AC/DC 電 源 大 幅
成長外， CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC 電 源 模 組 ， 亦 成 為 另 一 成
長動能 ， 投 資 評 等 為 Buy ，目標價為 2,050 元。

研 究 員 ： 王 美 珍   
iris.wang@capital.com.tw

電 容 器 更 換 事 件 對 營 運 影 響 有 限：  
市場流傳一份疑似台達 電 發給客戶的通知指出，特定批次電源供應器存在
潛 在 品 質 問 題 。內 容 表 示 根 據 調 查 ， 受 影 響 PSU 所 使 用 的 部 分 電 容 器 ， 在
未 施 加 電 壓、長 時 間 存 放 後，可 能 出 現 漏 電 流 增 加 情 況，特 定 條 件 下 恐 進 一
步 造 成 電 容 器 電 壓 不 平 衡 及 過 熱，重 新 上 電 時 可 能 導 致 電 容 器 故 障。此 次 涉
及台達 電 新一代 VR 平台 18.3 kW PSU ， 相 關 問 題 於 試 產 階 段 發 現 ， 台 達電
隨 後 通 知 客 戶 並 進 行 電 容 器 更 換 。 
台達 電 表 示 不 評 論 市 場 傳 聞；若 相 關 情 況 確 認 屬 供 應 商 零 組 件 問 題，公 司 將
依 實 際 狀 況 更 換 供 應 商 ， 並 配 合 客 戶 需 求 進 行 後 續 處 理 。 
此次 問 題 主 要 來 自 供 應 商 零 組 件，並 非 台 達 電 產 品 設 計，且 台 達電 擁 有 不 同
電 容 器 供 應 商 及 備 料 ， 預 期 可 透 過 更 換 電 容 器 供 應 來 源 解 決 。 
投 資 建 議 ：  
台達電 為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商， 受 惠 於 AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動
AC/DC 電 源 大 幅 成 長 外 ，CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC
電 源 模 組 ， 亦 成 為 另 一 成 長 動 能 。受 惠 於 AI 、 Data Center 領 域 需 求 成 長 ，
營 收 與 獲 利 維 持 成 長 趨 勢 ， 投 資 評 等 為Buy ， 目 標 價 為 2, 050 元。

相 關 個 股 獲 利 預 估 、 評 價 及 投 資 建 議

產業速報

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

個股報告

順德(2351) 
功率第一、均熱突圍，電源與散熱雙引擎同時引爆 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $410 
前次投資建議：2026.08.10 
「買進」 ，目標價: $252

報告日期：2026.09.10 研 究 員：邱岳霖

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ AI 導線架動能強勁，車市需求回溫，均熱片新案發酵，2026 年將重返榮耀，EPS 7.4元 
➢ AI 產品全面放量，均熱片進度優於預期，車用穩健成長，2027 年獲利倍增，上修 EPS 至
15.2 元，考量到今年獲利上修且未來長期營運動能強勁，維持「買進」投資評等，目標價調
升至 410 元(27X 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 72.6%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/9/9 收盤價： 237.50 ➢ 展望 2026 年，順德最大成長動能來自  AI 產品線，
目前逾 60 支新產品開案，在擴產效益帶動下，預期今
年 AI 相關營收倍數增長；均熱片近期已通過 4 間大
客戶認證，新增 4 台高噸位沖床與 15 台 CNC，並
年底達 100 萬片月產能、明年中達 2,000 萬片目標，
惟現階段量小、今年挹注不大。毛利率受惠  26H1 漲
價、稼動率高檔與產品組合轉佳，加上下半年客戶加價
搶產能，上修預估。綜上，兆豐投顧上修 2026年稅後
淨利 14.4 億元，YoY +374.4%，EPS 7.4 元。 
➢ 展望 2027 年，隨單機櫃功耗自數十 kW 往 100 kW 
以上，產業加速朝 HVDC 架構演進，順德憑藉與歐美
大廠合作開發的高功率、高散熱、高可靠度導線架成為
主要受惠者 ， AI 導線架營收可望隨  ± 400V/800V 
HVDC 滲透率提升而續強；均熱片進度優於研究部預
期，2,000 萬片有機會於 27 年中到位，隨 NPI 專案
逐步落地，27H2 動能將顯著增長，並隨 CPU/GPU 大
尺寸擴張成為新一輪成長主軸。在組合優化與稼動率維
持高檔下，毛利率可望續揚，兆豐投顧上修順德 2027 
年營收 206.4 億元(原估 155 億元)，YoY +45.7%，
毛利率 24.1%(原估 21.3%)，年增 2.8ppt，稅後淨利 
29.4 億元，YoY +104.5%，EPS 15.2 元(原估 8.4

- 2 - 
個股報告 
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

順德 2025年跨入 AI server領域，2026年散熱領域正式發酵 
順德(2351)成立於 1967 年，以辦公文具起家，1983 年跨入半導體導線架，
並於 2010 年與日本三菱伸銅(mitsubishi-shindoh)技術合作，取得導線架原
料銅合金製程設備及技術授權，目前產品涵蓋 IC 導線架、功率/分離式元件
導線架、LED 導線架、音圈馬達彈片，終端應用遍及車用、工業與消費性電
子。順德目前在全球導線架市佔排名前五，而在 Power IC 導線架則排名第
一。順德過往多以車用產品為主，不過隨著資料中心電源架構的改變，順德
與英飛凌將 800V 車用電源管理經驗移轉至資料中心，順德成為全台唯一 AI 
server 導線架主力供應商，並於 2025 年開始量產高功率導線架與穩壓器。
而順德自 2024 年布局均熱片跨足散熱領域，並於 2026 年開始生產 CPO 
Switch產品，而多家 CSP 之 CPU與 GPU 也有望於本年度驗證後開始出貨，
預期均熱片月產能從 26年底 100萬片大幅拉升至 27年 2,000萬片(不含 NPI
產品需求規劃)，反映 AI導線架與均熱片需求大幅增長，順德於中國江蘇廠、
台灣彰化廠與南投廠同步擴產，並新購大噸位沖床，同時也在台灣與馬來西
亞找地新建廠房以因應未來幾年高壓電源與均熱片之高速成長期。

- 3 - 
個股報告 
AI 導線架供不應求，輔以銅價上漲與客戶加價搶購，上修 26Q3 EPS至 2.3 
元 
順德(2351)公告 8月營收：營收 13.3 億元，MoM -0.1%/YoY +61.2%，為單
月次高，Q3達成率 69%，遠優研究部預期，未能再創高係因消費性包括 VCM
與文具類產品較上月有所衰退，不過預期在車用需求全面復甦與 AI 導線架
供不應求，Q3營收可望再創高至 36.5 億元，QoQ +3%/YoY +43.6%，車
用方面：除歐美車廠需求延續外，陸系新品也將推出，預期車用成長動能將
延續，此外 AI導線架於 Q2有近 30%季增長，預期在新產能開出下，營收將
逐月創高，而在產品組合優化與稼動率提升下，同時 AI 導線架供不應求使客
戶加價搶產能，故上修毛利率至 22%(原估 20%)，季增1.3ppt，年增9.6ppt，
同步上修稅後淨利 4.4 億元，QoQ +45.6%/YoY +405.2%，EPS 2.3 元 (原
估 1.8元)。

AI 導線架動能強勁，車市需求明顯回溫，均熱片新案持續發酵，2026 年將
重返榮耀，EPS 7.4元 
展望 2026 年，順德最大成長動能來自 AI 產品線，隨著伺服器電源架構改
變，PSU 瓦數持續提升，高功率導線架需求將被徹底激發，從高壓電網、
SST(固態變壓器)、Power Stage、48V bus converter、800V HVDC 與 BBU 
皆須用到高功率導線架，而順德自 2024 年攜手英飛凌將 800V 車用電源管
理經驗移轉至資料中心 ，使順德成為全球  AI server 導線架主力供應商之
一，目前有超過 60 支新產品開案，同時在擴產效益帶動下，預期 2026 年
AI 相關營收將倍數增長；在車用方面，受惠庫存調整結束，同時電動車需求
激增與新車款推出，預期全年呈雙位數增長；在均熱片方面，公司耕耘近兩
年，近期已通過 4 間大客戶認證，除新增 4 台高噸位沖床外也同步增加 15
台 CNC 設備，同時也於近期租賃新廠房以滿足年底 100 萬片月產能需求，
並於明年中達成 2,000 萬片產能目標，公司放眼價值更高 NPI，研究部認為
產業最具指標之共平面度，順德已能做至 30μm 以下，名列產業前茅，預期
將受客戶大幅青睞，更大尺寸產品用於 ASIC/CPU/GPU 均熱片有望陸續驗
證出貨，惟現階段出貨量較小且產能有限，預期今年對營運挹注不大。毛利
率方面：受惠 26H1 漲價帶動，稼動率維持高檔，同時產品組合轉佳下，且
下半年客戶加價搶產能 ，上修毛利率預估。綜上所述，兆豐投顧上修順德  
2026 年營收 142 億元，YoY +3 8.3%，毛利率 21.3%，年增 8ppt，稅後
淨利 14.4 億元，YoY +374.4%，EPS 7.4元。

AI 產品全面放量，均熱片進度優於預期，車用穩健成長，2027 年獲利倍增，
EPS 15.2 元 
展望 2027 年，隨著單機櫃功耗自數十 kW 往 100 kW 以上，過往 48V/54V 
供電架構已不敷使用，故產業正加速朝 HVDC(高壓直流)架構演進，順德憑
藉與歐美大廠合作開發的高功率、高散熱、高可靠度的導線架，將成為主要

作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出（預

高速增長；在均熱片方面，順德進度優於研究部預期，2,000 萬片有機會於

軸；車用則在電動化與在地化趨勢下維持穩健成長。在產品組合優化與稼動
率維持高檔下，毛利率可望續揚，兆豐投顧上修順德 2027 年營收 206.4 億

與長期營運，投資建議給 Buy 。  
輝達認購聯發科 ECB：輝達宣布斥資 35 億美元，認購聯發科的
ECB ，其餘 ECB 認購公司包含 Alphabet(GOOG US) 。  
聯發科將資金用於後續 AI 商機：聯發科此次募資資金將用於多
項用途，除了強化全球供應鏈外，也將持續加碼晶片設計、
ASIC 、互連 IP 以及先進封裝能力，藉此支援後續更大型的 AI
專案。  
雙方將有更多合作： 未來加強合作領域包含以下三點。一、 AI
基礎建設，聯發科將會基於 NVLink Fusion 生態系，協助客戶
開發基礎建設，並整合進 NVIDIA 的機櫃及 AI 工廠。二、邊緣
AI 運算，雙方會開發更多 RTX Spark 與 DGX Spark 的電腦晶
片。三、車用，雙方將持續開發具備 AI 功能的軟體定義汽車平
台。  
輝達投資聯發科，有助於聯發科 XPU 商機： 聯發科已與部分潛
在客戶展開接洽， NVIDIA 直接投資聯發科，可讓客戶更願意採
用聯發科 XPU 搭配 NVLink Fusion 的整體方案，也可讓雙方擴
大供應鏈與產品版圖。  
群益預估聯發科 2026 年 \2027 年稅後 EPS 60.57 元 \80.92 元：

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
環境保護：聯發科技做為全球半導體產業技術領導者，在整體價值鏈中位於
最前端的晶片設計，後端的原物料採購、代工製造、封裝與測試等環節皆交
由供應商執行，形成專業分工與互相合作的夥伴關係。根據風險調查與碳足
跡盤查發現，主要對社會與環境產生衝擊影響的關鍵在於後端製造作業，故
如何與供應商共同合作，以降低社會與環境相關的潛在風險，為聯發科技發
展綠色營運的首要任務。  
社會責任：運用數位科技解決社會問題不是企業的專利，聯發科技相信每個
人都有潛力利用數位科技創造無限可能，聯發科發起數位社會創新競賽 「智
在家鄉」，鼓勵社會大眾為家鄉問題提供解方。  
公司治理：聯發科技董事會下設有審計委員會，強化公司治理獨立性，並設
立薪資報酬委員會，以健全公司薪酬制度為目標，持續強化企業永續發展。
除前述法定功能性委員會外，本公司亦設立法定以外之併購策略委員會，視
實際需求針對集團具體併購案件及整體併購策略評估分析。  
產品組合：  
2Q26 產品組合：手機晶片業務 41% 、智慧裝置平台業務 53% 、 PMIC 6% 。  
手機業務全年展望保守：  
2Q26 手機晶片業務營收 QoQ-14%\YoY-20% ，營收占比 41% ，衰退原因是
手機的 BOM 表的成本上漲，聯發科預計 2026 年全球手機出貨量 YoY 約 -
15% 。  
客戶重視旗艦級產品與價格敏感的入門級產品 ， 聯發科 3Q26 將推出 2nm 製
程的 SoC ，為下一代 AI 模型提供動力，聯發科與全球廠商合作，持續擴大
2027 年旗艦級手機的市占率。  
供應鏈成本上漲已經是產業現象，聯發科將對價格進行調整，以確保成本上
漲可以反映在產品定價中。 3Q26 手機業務展望季持平或季減中個位數百分
比，旗艦級 SoC 的量產可以抵消大部分其他手機業務的衰退。  
客戶重視旗艦級產品與價格敏感的入門級產品 ， 聯發科 3Q26 將推出 2nm 製
程的 SoC ，為下一代 AI 模型提供動力，聯發科與全球廠商合作，持續擴大
2027 年旗艦級手機的市占率。  
智慧邊緣平台業務展望正向：  
2Q26 智慧邊緣平台業務營收 QoQ+19\YoY+26% ，營收占比 53%。成長動能
包含在運算、車用的市佔率的提升，以及 TV SoC 的成長。多項連接、車用
項目走向量產，聯發科預計此業務將季增中高個位數營收。  
2Q26 PMIC 營收占比約 6%：  
2Q26 PMIC 業務營收 QoQ+11%\YoY+6% ，營收占比 6%。成長動能包含資
料中心的市占率提升，聯發科預計此業務將季持平。

3 群益投顧 
AI ASIC 業務展望更樂觀：  
聯發科第一個 AI 加速器 ASIC 將於 4Q26 開始量產，預計 2026 全年資料中
心將貢獻超過 20 億美元，並且在 2027 年倍數成長，聯發科預計 AI ASIC 在
2027 年的市場規模將達到 800 億美元，聯發科目標市佔率從 10~15% ，上修
至 15~20% 。第二個 AI 加速器 ASIC 將於 2028 年初量產，聯發科有信心在
2028 年的市占率將提升。  
聯發科認為每代的 ASIC 的毛利率相當，並不會更高， ASIC 的毛利率低於
目前公司平均，但新世代的 ASIC 對營收的貢獻貴更大。  
其他補充：  
聯發科 448G SerDes 的研發順利，採用 CPO 的解決方案。 448G SerDes 之
後，聯發科會在台積電 (2330 TT) 的 COUPE 平台上，繼續發展 CPO 的解決
方案。聯發科還有提供 3.5D 的平台，該平台提供 IP、封裝與設計流程，適
用於大型資料中心的設計。  
供應鏈成本上漲已經是產業現象，聯發科將對價格進行調整，以確保成本上
漲可以反映在產品定價中。聯發科目前還在投入新技術，絕對費用金額還會
再增加，但是由於營收規模上升，費用率會下降。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Partnership expanded across AI infrastructure, local AI computing and n
automotive: Alongside the pricing, Nvidia and MediaTek announced a deepening of
their longstanding collaboration spanning three areas, including 1) AI
infrastructure: MediaTek will adopt Nvidia’s NVLink Fusion platform to enable
customers to develop custom AI infrastructure designed to integrate with Nvidia

rack-scale systems and AI factories. 2) Local AI computing: the companies will
continue to collaborate on multiple generations of RTX Spark™ and DGX Spark™ PC
chips integrating Nvidia GPUs with MediaTek SoCs. 3) Automotive: MediaTek and
Nvidia will continue developing platforms for AI-powered, software-defined vehicles
in the era of physical AI.
Key highlights from the joint interview
Following the announcement, the CEOs of Nvidia and MediaTek were interviewed jointly

by Bloomberg TV. Below we summarize key highlights from the interview.
Nvidia characterized the partnership with MediaTek as a multi-generation and n
long-term collaboration. Nvidia commented that the two companies are working
together across multiple generations of edge AI and PC SoCs, while the expanded
partnership will also cover data-center XPUs, networking and robotic systems.
Nvidia added that it is a decade-long roadmap.
The expanded collaboration will allow MediaTek’s XPUs to connect directly into n

Nvidia’s broader AI factory architecture. Nvidia noted that it will connect its
NVLink Fusion scale-up system, Spectrum-X switches and broader networking
infrastructure to MediaTek’s XPUs.  According to Nvidia, this would allow MediaTek
XPUs to be integrated into data centers already running Nvidia infrastructure, while
MediaTek XPU project wins could also create opportunities for Nvidia networking
products.
MediaTek expects the partnership to support growth in its AI ASIC business, n

although the new revenue contribution will take time. When asked whether the
collaboration could accelerate MediaTek’s AI chip business, MediaTek said that it
expects this to happen, but noted that it would take some time as the company is
only beginning to integrate Nvidia’s latest NVLink version into the relevant chassis.
MediaTek highlighted faster time-to-market for customers as a key benefit.
Both companies indicated that customer engagement around NVLink Fusion has n

already started. Nvidia said the collaboration was in large part driven by customer
requests and that there are already customers committed to NVLink Fusion.
Nvidia positioned NVLink Fusion as a way to accommodate custom XPUs within n
its existing infrastructure rather than as a replacement for its broader platform.
Nvidia noted that XPUs are already present in the market and said it is opening its
platform to allow customers’ specialized XPUs to connect more easily to Nvidia

infrastructure. Nvidia added that it expects to continue participating through
networking, switching and CPUs, even in AI factories where some racks incorporate
XPUs.

Goldman Sachs MediaTek (2454.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 3

Nvidia indicated that the partnership enables MediaTek to offer a more n
complete end-to-end XPU solution. Nvidia noted that an XPU provider would
otherwise need to address multiple system-level components, including NVLink
switching, NICs, Ethernet switching and scale-up/scale-out networking. Through the
partnership, these components can be provided through Nvidia’s existing
infrastructure.
MediaTek plans to use the bond proceeds across supply-chain and technology n

investments. MediaTek said supply-chain investment will be one use of proceeds
and emphasized that the company’s supply chain is global. The company also plans
to invest further in design capabilities, ASIC chip capabilities, IP and interconnects,
and advanced packaging, with the proceeds supporting the expanded Nvidia
collaboration.
The collaboration also extends to Nvidia’s custom HBM architecture. Nvidia n
indicated that its next-generation custom HBM will use an Nvidia-designed base

layer that has been extended to NVLink Fusion partners, including MediaTek and
Amazon. Nvidia noted that the NVHBM and XPU die will be integrated into a CoWoS
package, allowing partners to leverage Nvidia’s installed base and supply-chain
scale.
MediaTek identified the supply chain as its primary bottleneck. MediaTek said n
supply-chain challenges extend beyond HBM to substrates and other components,
while noting that its location in Taiwan and close collaboration with Nvidia are

helpful. Nvidia added that its networking ecosystem is becoming part of MediaTek’s
supply chain, while MediaTek’s XPU is becoming part of Nvidia’s supply chain.
Nvidia sees a solid standalone business case in its MediaTek investment. In n
response to questions on financing, Nvidia noted that MediaTek operates its own
independent business and is already a highly profitable and successful company.
Nvidia indicated the investment is grounded in the companies’ deep, multi-year

technology partnership, with the current roadmap extending over a decade, and is
intended to demonstrate Nvidia’s confidence in and commitment to the
collaboration. Nvidia also expects the investment to generate attractive returns.
Our thoughts - Strong ecosystem endorsement, broader system-level
opportunity and potential new TAM into custom HBM base die
Strong endorsement from two leading AI ecosystem partners
We believe the most immediate takeaway is the strong endorsement from both Nvidia

and Alphabet. Nvidia subscribed to US$3.5bn of MediaTek’s US$3.9bn CB issuance,
while Alphabet and global institutional investors participated in the remaining
US$400mn. In our view, Nvidia’s capital commitment provides strong validation of
MediaTek’s silicon, connectivity and advanced packaging capabilities. Alphabet’s
participation also reinforces MediaTek’s relevance across multiple major AI ecosystems.
Funding should strengthen supply-chain security and support continued technology

investment. According to the company, one of the original purposes of the financing
was to secure capacity and supply for constrained components such as HBM.
Although certain customers currently procure HBM and other components directly, we
1 September 2026   3
Goldman Sachs MediaTek (2454.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

believe MediaTek could assume greater supply-chain responsibility under future
engagement models, including entering into capacity or supply agreements on behalf of
customer projects.
The company also indicated that it plans to invest further in its technology and design
capabilities, including ASIC design, IP , interconnects and advanced packaging, etc. We
believe the additional funding should provide MediaTek with greater flexibility to
secure the resources required for increasingly complex AI related programs.

The partnership broadens MediaTek’s role from custom AI silicon toward system-level
enablement
Based on the interview and the company announcement, we believe MediaTek is seeking
to extend its role beyond designing and delivering an individual AI ASIC toward helping
customers address a broader portion of the system surrounding the XPU. This could
include connectivity, memory architecture, advanced packaging, manufacturing and
integration with rack-scale AI infrastructure.

However, we do not believe this means MediaTek intends to build or sell complete
AI racks. Rather, MediaTek can help customers integrate their custom XPUs into
Nvidia’s pre-validated chassis, networking and rack-scale architectures, allowing
customers to deploy and scale their AI infrastructure more efficiently.
NVLink Fusion is intended to provide customers with a faster path from custom XPU to
AI factory. Customers can focus their resources on the differentiated compute portion of

the accelerator while relying on Nvidia and MediaTek for surrounding technologies such
as NVLink connectivity, high-speed SerDes, memory architecture, packaging,
manufacturing and system qualification.
Instead of independently engineering and qualifying every component surrounding the
custom XPU, customers can use NVIDIA ’s prebuilt, prequalified and system-prevalidated
platform as a starting point. We believe this should reduce integration complexity and

risk, shorten time to market and make it easier for customers to deploy custom XPUs
within existing Nvidia-based data centers and scale them into rack-level AI factories.
Incremental revenue will likely take time, but the partnership expands MediaTek’s
longer-term opportunity
MediaTek and Nvidia did not provide a timeline for the first NVLink Fusion-related
customer, product or revenue contribution. The company indicated that it is only
beginning to integrate the latest version of NVLink, after which customers would still

need to complete their own product development, qualification and deployment
processes. We therefore see limited immediate earnings implications and believe the
incremental opportunity is more likely to emerge over the medium to long term.
Nevertheless, the partnership broadens MediaTek’s addressable opportunity from
designing an individual AI ASIC toward enabling a production-ready system. We
believe this could improve customer win rates and increase MediaTek’s potential content

and value capture per project through additional contributions from connectivity,
memory architecture, advanced packaging, manufacturing and system integration.

### Page 5

The relationship extends across the full edge-to-cloud AI computing stack 
Beyond AI infrastructure, the partnership covers multiple generations of DGX Spark and 
RTX Spark chips for local AI computing, as well as automotive platforms. We therefore 
view the announcement as a broader, multi-generation technology alignment spanning 
cloud AI factories, enterprise and local AI computing, PCs and physical AI, rather than a 
single-product collaboration. 
Management noted that the Nvidia partnership should not restrict MediaTek’s existing 
custom ASIC engagements. We see NVLink Fusion as an additional architecture option 
rather than an exclusive platform. Customers will be able to decide whether to adopt the 
Nvidia ecosystem depending on their infrastructure and workload requirements, while 
MediaTek can continue supporting customers using other architectures. 
In our view, Alphabet’s participation in the CB issuance should allay any concerns 
some investors may have had that the closer Nvidia relationship could affect 
MediaTek’s existing hyperscaler engagements. Instead, we believe MediaTek’s 
positioning is to remain architecture-flexible while providing customers with a broader 
range of design and system-integration options. 
NVHBM collaboration extends MediaTek’s participation into the memory and 
packaging architecture 
We note that Nvidia’s next-generation NVHBM will use a custom HBM base layer 
designed by Nvidia, which has also been extended to NVLink Fusion partners including 
MediaTek and Amazon. Nvidia noted that NVHBM will be integrated with the XPU die in 
a CoWoS package, allowing partners to leverage NVIDIA ’s broader HBM and packaging 
supply chain. While the NVHBM base layer itself is NVIDIA-designed, we believe 
MediaTek’s participation could deepen its involvement in memory architecture, 
interconnect and advanced packaging integration for XPU programs. Separately, 
MediaTek has highlighted custom HBM and memory-interface capabilities as part 
of its broader XPU offering, which we believe could provide incremental content 
opportunities for customer-specific designs outside Nvidia’s standardized NVHBM 
implementation. 
Investment Thesis, Price Target Risks and Methodology

Investment Thesis - MediaTek 
MediaTek is a leading global IC design house specializing in smartphone AP (application 
processor). We have a positive stance on MediaTek, viewing it as well-positioned to 
transition from a traditional smartphone application provider to an AI-focused vendor, 
beginning with AI smartphones and extending to enterprise ASICs and smart automotive 
solutions (in partnership with NVIDIA) in 2025 and beyond. We expect MediaTek to 
achieve solid multi-year growth, where we expect revenue and earnings to increase by 
43%/59% CAGRs, respectively, in 2025-28E. This growth will be primarily driven by: 1) 
market share gains, particularly in the premium segment (specifically high-end 5G 
flagship SoCs), 2) strong ramp in AI ASIC business, and 3) new TAM in the 
automotive/computing sectors.

Elite Material
Valuation
Our target price for EMC is set at NT$6,000, based on a target PE multiple of 30x on our 2027E EPS average. We believe our
target PE multiple, at the peak of its forward PE average in the past 5 years, is justified by a strong earnings outlook supported
by its leading position in CCL supply for AI GPU and ASIC (high m/s) from 2026E onwards. We also believe the continued spec

upgrade for CCL and price hikes should help support its margin expansion going forward. At our target price, the shares would
trade at 23.6x/13.2x 2026E/27E PB.
Risks
Citi’s quant system rates EMC high-risk given high share price volatility, which we largely attribute to frequent debates over the
spec upgrade for next-gen AI servers. In general, we believe some investors initially tend to have high hope for aggressive spec
upgrade without sufficiently considering the scalability and cost structure of CCL products, which then leads to a shortfall in

the end. However, from a longer-term perspective, we see a decent and clear spec upgrade trend with meaningful ASP growth
potential among AI servers of different generations. As such, we do not assign a high-risk rating.
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) a slower-than-expected CCL
upgrade trend; 2) weaker-than-expected AI server demand; 3) production bottlenecks in AI supply chain (eg, glass, copper foil,

foundry or OSAT); 4) unexpected share loss in key AI server projects; and 5) stricter practice of OOC policy by end customers.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

For the month of September 2026, we estimate AirTAC’s monthly revenue will increase 
to Rmb827mn (+6% MoM, +17% YoY)/NT$3,930mn (+6% MoM, +27% YoY) 
We expect September revenue will go up by 6% MoM to Rmb827mn/NT$3,930mn, 
mainly driven by the improvement in daily pneumatic revenue (Rmb30.9mn in Sep vs. 
Rmb 29.1mn in Aug) despite the same number of working days. The improvement in 
daily pneumatic revenue is driven by the strong growth from key customers, particularly 
in electronics (we also expect peers to see strong demand in electronics, see here), 
followed by batteries and machine tools, and we expect demand to improve following 
the inventory pull-in after the Europe summer holiday.

Investment view: Strong YoY momentum in electronics could drive overall growth. 
Maintain positive view on AirTAC 
We reiterate our Buy rating on AirTAC, as we continue to see key end-segments 
performance tracking ahead of the company’s 2026 full year guidance of 20%+ YoY

### Page 3

growth (see here), with electronics/battery/machine tools delivering 20%/64%/31% 
YTD YoY growth while total revenue in Rmb is tracking at 25% YTD YoY growth. The 
strong YTD performance is in line with our expectation that AirTAC is a key beneficiary 
of the factory automation/industrial automation localization trend in mainland China, as 
well as benefiting from the prolonged upcycle supported by government stimulus 
policies. However, we maintain caution on the potential of exaggerated orders (including 
advance or duplicate orders), and will continue to monitor the underlying end-demand 
closely. 
Furthermore, we believe the company is well-positioned to continue to gain market 
share in the China pneumatic market, particular given its pricing strategy, (1) its 
pneumatic products pricing remain competitively priced at 10%+ below international 
peers, and (2) the company has maintained stable pricing in China (China accounts for 
~95% of total revenue in 2026E per GSe) amid the inflationary cost environment, while 
the company will continue to enhance internal operational efficiency to mitigate the 
rising cost risk (see here). As a result, we continue to see backlog accumulating and 
expect the company to maintain a high UTR to digest these orders. 
Maintain Buy on AirTAC with an unchanged 12m TP of NT$1,925. 
Investment Thesis - AirTAC 
AirTAC is the 2nd-largest pneumatic supplier in mainland China with 28% market share 
in 2025, outpacing most local competitors, owing to its low-cost yet high quality 
product, and service quality reputation in most key cities in mainland China. We forecast 
its market share will reach 30% in 2026E and expect AirTAC to be the structural winner 
in a deflationary market, due to its: (1) strong market growth and leadership: AirTAC 
continues to expand its market share in mainland China driven by competitive pricing 
that is more than 10% lower vs. international peers (with 45%+ GM / 30-35% OPM) and 
high demand elasticity among mainland China customers; and (2) superior cost structure 
and financial health: with best-in-class cash cost and a solid balance sheet, featuring 
positive net cash over FCF since 2023/2024, we believe AirTAC is well-equipped to 
weather industry downturns and capitalize on market restructuring. 
Overall, we believe AirTAC will see better revenue/profitability in the long term given its 
share gainer position in the China pneumatic market and potential to gain share in the 
linear guide market (improved demand in the next 1-2 years). This should lead to 
strengthened valuation, vs. its recent trading range (trading below its 10-year avg. P/E 
multiple). We are Buy rated on AirTAC. 
Price target risks and methodology - AirTAC 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,925 is based on a target P/E of 26.0x on 
our 2027E EPS (0.25x STDV above avg of the last pneumatic upcycle). 
Key downside risks: (1) Stronger competition from peers; (2) sharper-than-expected 
Rmb depreciation, and (3) a longer-than-expected end-market demand downtrend in 
mainland China.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Document 084: 260906_ubs_largan-DG

### Page 1

ab 6 September 2026
Global Research
Largan Precision
Strong CPO ramp priced ahead of qualification
The re-rating has outpaced qualification progress; downgrade from Buy to Sell
Largan shares have risen 242% since April on optimism around its CPO FAU progress.
While we stay constructive on Largan's CPO prospects, we believe the market is pricing
in confidence ahead of still-early qualification progress, particularly given a crowded
competitive field. Largan only starts its sample line late Q3, integrating its Prism Micro

Lens Array and Fiber Array with AOET's V-groove and Corning's fiber. We expect  several
quarters to improve yields, gain integrator acceptance (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and
convert its initial production-oriented FA program into volume while competing against
incumbent TFC and vertically integrated rivals incl. Coherent. We downgrade our rating
from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-
18x range well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 PT based on 15x smartphone lens

+ 50x CPO base case for 40% share  (upper end of optical peers  at 14-72x).
Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low) and
1 FA design in an FAU. A risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028

and also competes with incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and
other FAU vendors like Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not
requiring Largan's initial V-groove approach. Largan's push toward a complete solution
may also conflict with integration partners.
Core smartphone lens tracking behind amid memory headwinds
We also see risk on near-term results with Q326 sales tracking light. Aug sales were NT
$5bn, +3% MoM, -16% YoY, taking QTD revenue to NT$10.1bn, 53% of UBSe/Street

Q326. Sept would need to grow +73% MoM to meet UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ vs.
normal seasonal +8% MoM. We expect flattish builds at best for its flagship customer
(80% of sales) needing to raise price to pass on memory costs +US$200-300 YoY albeit
some support from high-end introducing variable-aperture lens, while foldable lens
specs remain similar. Factoring muted smartphone growth but healthy margins, we view
a mid-teens multiple appropriate for this business, in line with its historical range.

Stock prices in our upside case ahead of traction; risk-reward skews negative
We maintain basic '26E/27E EPS at NT$190/NT$214 and NT$5,000 SOTP PT based on 1)
15x smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730
(40% share, 100k switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of
peers due to its premium ~40-45% OpM. We view risk/reward skewed negative with
downside to NT$2,500 on limited CPO success and softer smartphones, base case NT

$5,000 for CPO traction, and upside 22% if the stock is bid to our upside case NT$9,000
based on top-end 70x P/E on CPO earnings at 60% share of a higher US$1.58bn TAM.

### Page 2

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 2
Largan Precision UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Could Largan's CPO products contribute meaningful revenue in a few years?
We stay constructive but the company still has hurdles to clear in a crowded space. Its scale and track
record in high-end smartphone lens gives it an opportunity supplying key components of the CPO

FAU assembly, traditional strength in precision lens micro assemblies, alignment accuracy, in-house
tooling and automation that could give it a differentiated solution. Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan
suggests the company is still early in qualification, with its sample production line for customer
evaluation readied in late September and still requiring work with a system integrator and
qualification with the fabless customer and ODMs.  We still see a year to move from sampling to

design-in but if successful timed to meet expected market acceleration in 2028-29.
Q: Will there be further technology upgrades driving the lens cycle in the coming years?
We expect spec upgrades for Largan's core lens business to come in H226 from variable aperture
shipments for the iPhone 18 (which Genius does not currently participate though may enter over
time, as seen with periscope lenses). Key offset though is weaker sales with Q3 tracking light and

suggesting a softer build due to memory headwinds. Further upside could come from broader
adoption of 48MP modules in future iPhone models. Looking longer term, Apple is reportedly
exploring 200MP periscope lens and adjacent opportunities to penetrate physical AI categories with
precision lens including robotics, autonomous systems and AR/VR devices.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell as we view the market expectations as now
pricing in a bullish scenario for strong market traction with the company still at an early stage of

qualification, still needing to improve its process and yields, and competing in a crowded space also
against an existing incumbent. We expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain
acceptance from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI GPU
fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and other rivals. We downgrade
Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x

range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens +
50x CPO base case for40% share  (upper-end of optical peer range of 14-72x).
Largan has demonstrated its samples with partners at Computex and Semicon Taiwan and cited its
goals to ramp CPO as its next opportunity. The company is still early in qualifications with a sample
automated line in the coming months followed by design-in engagement with system integrators,

ODMs and the fabless customer to target design-in on 3rd gen CPO solutions in 2028. For
smartphone lens, its top customer had a strong iPhone 17 cycle although acknowledged it would
have to raise price to pass on higher memory costs which could dampen growth. Sales QTD are
tracking slightly light as Largan also competes with 2nd source Genius. Our supply chain checks and
management commentary points to introduction of higher value variable aperture lens in H226.

Largan is currently trading above the high end of its historical range at 39x/35x our 2026E/27E EPS vs.
a long-term range of 13-18x forward consensus earning, pricing in success penetrating the CPO
component market ahead of the earnings stream which would only start late in 2027 into 2028. We
note optical component peers trade at 14-72x with an average of ~38x 2027E P/E (Genius at 25x
2027 P/E). We maintain our SOTP valuation with our price target at NT$5000 based on 1) 15x

smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730 (40% share, 100k
switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of peers due to its premium ~40-
45% OpM. We view the stock at NT$7,400 now pricing in CPO traction on a 50x multiple of our
upside NT$76 case based on 60% share of an upside US$1.6bn opportunity.

### Page 3

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 3
Largan Precision UBS Research
commentary
Largan shares have risen 242% since 1 April on optimism around its CPO fiber array unit
(FAU) progress. While we stay constructive post our feedback at Semicon Taiwan on
Largan's ability to develop CPO solutions, we now believe the market is pricing in a level
of confidence that has run well ahead of qualification progress which is still early and
competing in a crowded field. Largan will only start its sample line in late Q3 integrating

its Prism Micro Lens Array and Fiber Array, AOET's V-groove and Corning's glass fiber.
From there, we expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain acceptance
from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI
GPU fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and rivals including
Coherent with own lens/FAU. The company did note on its Q226 call that it had secured
one mass production commitment for its fiber array to an FAU maker that could ramp by

H227 although early to conclude the magnitude of this engagement or end customer
design-in, with the other customers still at proof-of-concept phase.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and
the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT
$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens + 50x CPO base case for 40% share
(upper-end of optical component peers trading at a  range of 14-72x).

Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low). A
risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028 and also competes with

incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and other FAU vendors like
Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not requiring Largan's initial V-
groove approach. Largan's business model may also conflict with integration partners as
it also attempts to build toward a complete solution.
We estimate Largan's Total Addressable Market in a downside case is US$270mn if it
only supplies sub-components into a market that continues to push out CPO due to

reliability concerns relative to pluggables and NPO/XPO options, US$1.08bn in a base
case with 100k switches and 72 FAUs per switch at US$150 ASP , and US$1.6bn in an
upside case with 125k switches, 72 FAUs per switch and US$175 ASP .  For Largan, we
estimate downside case 10% share yields NT$810mn sales and NT$2.20 EPS, base case
40% share, NT$13.0bn sales and NT$35 EPS, and upside case 60% share, NT$28.4bn
sales and NT$76 EPS.
Although we are optimistic on the market opportunity scaling up and Largan's

competencies and progress developing these solutions, Largan's traction is not yet
certain and it will compete in a crowded landscape. We believe Himax is already
supplying wafer level optics for the collimator function with FOCI (3363.TWO) and TFC
(300394.SZ) already has demonstrated capability and design-in on the FAU and lens
assembly. Others that could vertically integrate include Coherent, Senko, Corning and
Sumitomo, and we also note Genius sending samples and Sunny Optical's intention to

develop for this market.

Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan indicates Coherent can make the FAU and lens by itself
through capabilities merging with II-VI and demonstrated all in-house components at
the booth except for the metal transceiver casing.

Smartphone lens business tracking behind, should have a mild 2026-27
Largan reported August sales at NT$5bn,   is below seasonal 63-67% at only 53% of
Q326 UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ and down 16% YoY, suggesting muted momentum
into its flagship customer (~80% of sales). July EPS was NT$13.94, 27% of UBSe Q3 NT
$51.52 suggesting in-line GM/OpM at ~50%/39% but the lighter sales are more of a

concern.
UBS expects flattish builds at best and a mild spec upgrade with one model variable
aperture but foldable at similar lens spec and higher memory cost (+US$200-300 YoY).
We still see mild growth and mid-teens multiple acceptable for the smartphone
business, similar to its long-term average.

Largn (3008.TW) trading at 7,400 at closed Sept 4.

UPSIDE (NT$9,000): Our upside valuation of NT$3,655 is based on  its average P/E of 16x
off our 2027 upside case EPS of NT$235. We factor in faster than expected
CPO progress to deliver upside EPS of NT$76 and 70x P/E at the high end of optical peers
trading at 14-72x 2027E P/E to an implied valuation of NT$5,345, and  total share price at
NT$9,000.
BASE (NT$5,000):  Our price target for its smartphone business is NT$3,270, with a 15x
2027 P/E applied on its base case EPS of NT$214 slightly below the long-term average of

its historical P/E band to reflect the macro uncertainty. Paired with our base scenario of its
CPO business at NT$35 EPS and 50x P/E, we arrive at an implied valuation of NT$1,730,
and Largan's aggregate PT  at NT$5,000.
DOWNSIDE (NT$2,500):   Downside valuation at NT$2,392, based on low-end of its
range 13x 2027 P/E. We take into consideration slower adoption of FAUs at its downside
EPS of NT$2 and the same 50x P/E to  an implied valuation of NT$108 for

CPO contribution, and Largan's total share price at NT$2,500.

Forecast returns
Forecast price appreciation -32.4%
Forecast dividend yield 1.1%
Forecast stock return -31.4%
Market return assumption 6.3%
Forecast excess return -37.6%
Company Description
Founded in 1987, Largan Precision (Largan) is one of the world's leading manufacturers of
lenses used in cameras and IT products. It specialises in plastic/hybrid lenses and, to a lesser
extent, glass lenses and modules. In 2022, 20MP+ camera lenses were 10-20% of Largan's

shipment mix, 10MP lenses were 50-60%, 8MP lenses were 0-10% and other lenses were
30-40%.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on SOTP valuation on the CPO opportunity and traditional lens
business.
We think Largan's downside risks include: 1) ongoing pricing pressure from smartphone OEM
clients and increasingly intense competition among lens suppliers; and 2) a slower-than-
expected smartphone demand recovery in China, and 3) CPO commercial success below the

market's elevated expectations.
We believe the key upside risk is a faster-than-expected CPO qualification process at Largan.
In addition, if the company demonstrates exceptionally efficient and highly accurate
automation capabilities, it could support greater market share gains within the industry than
we currently anticipate.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

Accton Technology Corporation  | Asia Pacific
August Sales +4% MoM/+59%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Accton's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for share price movement.
Details:
• August sales were NT$40,873mn (+4% MoM/+59% YoY).
• QTD reached 66% of our 3Q26 estimate, at NT$120,883mn (+27% QoQ/
+66% YoY), and 70% of consensus, at NT$114,321mn (+20% QoQ/+57%
YoY).
Our view:
• We see 3Q26 revenue tracking in line with our estimates

• Our checks indicate that Accton has been negotiating with customers to let
them consign  those expensive components, which could weigh on its
revenue growth but should be positive to margins.
• Looking ahead, we expect the momentum to remain elevated into 2H26, as it
should benefit from broader-based migration to 800G network switches
from both existing and new customers, and the ramp of the new AI
accelerator model from its major customer should be in full swing.

• Stay OW with a PT of NT$3,500, based on 30x 2027 P/E.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Accton Technology Corporation (2345.TW)
Base case, P/E, which we believe better captures the company's earnings growth momentum.
We assign a target P/E of 30x to our 2027 EPS estimate, which we view as justified by an
operating profit CAGR of 48% for 2025-28, based on our estimates. This implies a PEG of
around 0.6, vs. its average of 0.7 over 2019-25, when the stock was trading at a P/E of 22x for

an operating profit CAGR of 32%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger data center capex
n Faster network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand losing share in AI networks
n Stronger custom AI accelerator growth
n Stronger ODM direct business model adoption
Risks to Downside
n Weaker data center capex
n Slower network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand taking more share in AI networks
n Softer custom AI accelerator growth
n Softer ODM direct business model adoption

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
August  Sales  +4% MoM/+156%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Chroma's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for share price movement.
Details:
• August revenue was NT$5,632mn (+4% MoM/+156% YoY).
• QTD revenue reached 75% of MSe, at NT$14,772mn (+9% QoQ/+130% YoY),
and 79% of consensus, at NT$13,992mn (+3% QoQ/+118% YoY).
Our view:
• We see 3Q26 tracking ahead of market expectations and likely to deliver

over 20% QoQ.
• Looking ahead, we expect its power testing business to stay at an elevated
level, and the semiconductor & photonics business to continue growing HoH
into 2H26, driven by SLT, metrology, CPO and potential shipment of FT
handlers/burn-in ovens.
• Stay OW.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
Base case, 45x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it better
captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average valuation of
~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating profit CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG of 1.0. We think
our target multiple of 45x is justified by the 61% operating profit CAGR we project for

2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected macro conditions
n Stronger semi and photonics and power testing demand
n Margin improvement via better product mix
n Better opex control
Risks to Downside
n Macro slowdown
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower progress in the advanced packaging capacity expansion
n Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand
n Margin contraction arising from lack of revenue scale and increasing opex

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total

Greater China Semiconductors  | Asia Pacific
Quantum Security / eMemory:
Investor Feedback
Low investor expectations, mostly a teach-in – eMemory
remains under-owned and under-researched, so feedback was
largely educational rather than push-back; we see this as the
marketing opportunity.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst
Daniel.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2863

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
What we published – and the feedback. On 17 August we released the Global
Foundation “Hardware Quantum Security – Building Defenses for Q-Day and
Beyond” with our eMemory OW initiation (PT NT$3,330): as quantum computing
nears (base case ~2030), a new hardware-security layer must be deployed 3-5 years
ahead, with physical unclonable function (PUF)-based Root-of-Trust IP adding 5-10%

to the semi IP market. Feedback was constructive – clients agree the “Harvest Now,
Decrypt Later” risk and 2030-31 migration deadlines make this a multi-year, non-
binary theme. The recurring point –  and our marketing opportunity – is that  Aspeed
is already well-owned while eMemory stays under-researched by global funds.
Debate 1 – sizing the TAM. The key question is whether PUF is truly required once
post-quantum cryptography (PQC) arrives. We size a PUF-PQC TAM of ~US$310mn

by 2030 (base), US$600mn bull / US$190mn bear, on ~25% PQC adoption and
~50% PUF attach, with datacenter/AI servers the largest royalty pool. Once PQC is
implemented, secure key generation/storage makes PUF the logical anchor – so
sensitivity sits on PQC pace, not PUF attach. We view eMemory’s NeoPUF as best-in-
class: near-zero bit-error rate, lower area/power, and superior advanced-node scaling
vs. SRAM PUF.
Debate 2 – is eMemory the right proxy? Yes. The case rests on the core royalty

engine: royalties are the majority of earnings and tied to customer production, so as
mix shifts to leading-edge nodes, higher wafer values lift royalty per design-win.
Advanced-node projects are already >50% of licensing  non-recurring engineering
(NRE) revenue. This is corroborated by the just-reported August sales of NT
$357mn, +42.8% Y/Y (8M26 +21.9% Y/Y, 8th straight month of growth);
royalties were 60.8% of 2Q26 revenue (+7.8% Y/Y) and management guides for

royalty growth to accelerate into 2H26 on advanced-node ASPs and new apps (OTP,
NeoFlash/1T Flash, PUF-PQC). (Continued in the next section)
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Under-owned, mostly a teach-in: feedback was largely educational, not push-back
– the marketing opportunity; clients called the level “a good entry point”.
August sales momentum: NT$357mn, +42.8% Y/Y; 8M26 +21.9% Y/Y (8th
straight month of Y/Y growth).
Reiterate Overweight, PT NT$3,330: ~30% further upside, plus free optionality

on a US$310mn base-case PUF-PQC TAM by 2030.
September 8, 2026 09:00 PM GMT

### Page 2

 Idea
2

Our view and stock calls. Reiterate OW, PT NT$3,330 (~30% upside from NT$2,570) – a 
rare mix of a defensive, high-visibility royalty stream (100% gross margin, ~63% ROE) plus 
free optionality on the post-quantum build-out, alongside Synopsys (OW) and adopter 
Aspeed (OW). Catalysts: monthly-sales momentum, the advanced-node royalty ramp, PQC 
design-win news-flow and San Jose OCP in Oct 2026. Risks: 1) semi downturn, 2) PUF-PQC 
competition, 3) slower quantum/PQC adoption. Net-net, eMemory is an under-owned 
name where the royalty re-rating and quantum-security theme are both intact.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
eMemory Technology Inc (3529.TWO)
We derive our price target from a residual income model. We assume an 8.6% cost of equity
(2.0% risk-free rate, 6.0% equity risk premium, and 1.1 beta), a payout ratio of 90%, a medium-
term earnings growth rate of 23.0%, and a terminal growth rate of 4.4%. Based on these
assumptions, our RI model implies an equity value of NT$249bn, or NT$3,330 per share.

Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected PUF adoption
n Stronger-than-expected advanced-node OTP migration
n Higher-than-expected royalty expansion from wafer value growth
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected adoption by key customers
n Slower-than-expected PQC adoption
n Increasing geopolitical and trade policy risks
Aspeed Technology (5274.TWO)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions:
n Cost of equity of 9.8% (2.0% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.3 beta)

n Medium-term growth rate of 21.4%
n Terminal growth rate of 5.5%
n Cash payout ratio of 85%
Risks to Upside
n Stronger cloud demand
n Faster-than-expected spec migration
n Mild competition
Risks to Downside
n Softening cloud demand
n Slower-than-expected spec migration
n Intensified competition
n Further policy tightening in China
Synopsys Inc. (SNPS.O)
The sector is trading on a 30-35x P/E multiple range. We expect Synopsys to trade in line and

apply a c.33x P/E multiple to estimated FY26 EPS ($15.12).
Risks to Upside
n Bigger ramp-up in AI ASICs and GPUs than modelled
n Quicker recovery in Automotive & industrial
n Design wins at expense of peers with Ansys products

n Strong recovery in IP business (esp. in China)
Risks to Downside
n Scale and duration of downturn seeing deeper R&D cuts
n Further tightening of export controls
n Loss of share to new entrant EDA players given localisation trends
n Failure to hit cost synergies from Ansys merger

Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Document 106: 260909_daiwa_ardentec-buy-initiation

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Investment case: We initiate coverage of Ardentec, a Taiwanese pure 
testing provider serving foundries, IDMs and fabless chipmakers, with a 
Buy (1) rating. We believe Ardentec is transitioning from an auto/industrial 
test vendor into an AI/HPC wafer-sort powerhouse, supported by a 35% 
revenue CAGR (2025-28E) and gross margin expansion to 45% by 2028E.

Longtan fab ramp with premium hourly rate. We expect the 7-story 
Longtan mega-fab, which is developing at one floor per quarter to deliver 
significant revenue contribution (c.TWD450m+/quarter per floor). We 
believe this underpins a structural upgrade in revenue, as advanced wafer 
sorting for application-specific integrated circuits (ASIC) commands an 
hourly billing rate nearly double that of legacy testing. Furthermore, we 
believe strategic collaborations with key Taiwan foundry leaders position 
Ardentec to capture expanding AI/HPC test allocations moving forward.

Gross margin increase driven by high utilisation rate. As its key peers 
prioritise merchant AI GPUs, displaced mobile/RF/networking orders are 
refilling Ardentec’s fully depreciated tester lines, lifting mature utilisation. 
Test-time inflation (2-3x on complex ASICs) anchors Longtan capacity at a 
90%+ utilisation rate. As fixed costs comprise 40-45% of COGS, this drives 
blended gross margin from 36% in 2025 to 45% in 2028E, with steady 
operating expense (9-10% of sales) flowing into operating profit.

Turnkey E/O testing for CPO and non-dilutive expansion. Leveraging 
synergies with Giga-Solution, Raytek (7873 TT; 25% stake), and strategic 
partner Fabrinet (FN US, NR), Ardentec builds a comprehensive electro-
optical test flow aligned with TSMC’s compact universal photonic engine 
(COUPE) platform. While co-packaged optics (CPO) testing accounts for 
<5% of near-term sales, it secures an early-mover moat in optical I/O.

Catalysts: 1) Completion of 7-floor capacity with premium rates and higher 
UTR; 2) peer spillover orders refilling fully depreciated capacity, expanding 
gross margin; 3) higher-than-expected ASIC and CPO contribution.

Valuation: Driven by a structural earnings inflection fuelled by Longtan fab 
expansion, expanding AI ASIC test pricing premiums, and high-margin peer 
spillover orders, we initiate Ardentec with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP 
of TWD340, based on a target PER of 25x applied to our 4-quarter-forward 
EPS, at the top end of its historical 10-25x range but supported by a 48% 
2025-28E EPS CAGR and peer AI test valuations of 18-24x.

Risks: 1) Delays in capacity expansion; 2) pricing pressure as a second 
supplier; and 3) slow automotive and industrial recovery.

9 September 2026

Ardentec

➢ Pure testing captures ASIC growth and order spillover from peers

### Page 3

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026

How do we justify our view? 
Growth outlook Valuation Earnings revisions

Growth outlook

Ardentec: revenue and gross margin outlook   
We forecast Ardentec to enter a structural multi-year 
revenue growth cycle, rising from TWD14.0bn in 2025 to 
TWD34.4bn by 2028E, implying a 34.8% CAGR. This 
revenue growth is supported by the phased ramp-up of its 
Longtan fab facility, increasing exposure to premium AI 
ASIC and HPC wafer testing, and broad-based ASP 
expansion driven by tighter mature-node test capacity. 
Meanwhile, we expect improving utilisation coupled with 
growing contributions from fully depreciated legacy testers, 
to drive gross margin expansion from 36.3% in 2025 to 
45.0% by 2028E.

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts

Valuation   Ardentec: 1-year-forward PER bands    
Given the current structural mix transition from cyclical 
automotives towards high-margin AI/HPC custom ASICs, 
amplified gross margin and operating leverage from the 
Longtan ramp, and ROE inflection likely to surpass its 2022 
historical peak of c.23% over 2026-28E, we initiate 
coverage of Ardentec with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month 
TP of TWD340, based on a target PER of 25.0x applied to 
our 4-quarter-forward EPS, at the top end of its historical 
10-25x range but supported by a 48% 2025-28E EPS 
CAGR and peer AI test valuations of 18-24x.

Source: Bloomberg

Earnings revisions

Ardentec: Bloomberg-consensus earnings revisions    
Bloomberg consensus raised its 2026-27E EPS estimates 
by 30-45%, driven by the rapid Longtan Floor 1 ramp, 
premium ASIC hourly rates, and high-margin peer spillover 
orders. The upward revisions likely reflect the completion 
of the remaining floors, test-time inflation driving higher 
testing hours, and internal capacity tightness unlocking 
pricing power across mature lines. Ardentec is currently 
covered by 4 brokers. We believe the market is overlooking 
this stock despite its structural fundamental turnaround 
outlook and depressed valuation.

Source: Bloomberg

Company profile 
Kinsus was founded in 2000 as a subsidiary of Asustek. The company mainly focus on niche ball grid 
array (BGA) substrates to avoid price competition among mainstream products like graphics card and 
chipsets, and it has become top-3 flip chip manufacturer in global. Besides, its manufacturing sites are 
based in Taiwan and China.

### Page 6

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
From Automotive to AI/HPC growth engines 
A premier pure play in wafer testing 
Founded in October 1999 and headquartered in Hukou, Hsinchu, Ardentec Corporation is 
a premier semiconductor testing pure play, ranking among the top three pure test service 
providers in Taiwan in terms of revenue. While semiconductor testing is inherently a 
capital- and technology-intensive arena dominated by foundry and integrated OSAT titans 
such as TSMC (2330 TT, TWD2,465, Buy [1]; covered by Daiwa analyst Rick Hsu) and 
ASE Group (3711 TT, TWD640, Outperform [2]; covered by Daiwa analyst Rick Hsu), 
Ardentec establishes a resilient, differentiated market presence through two structural 
pillars:

• Strategic dual-sourcing anchor and supply chain balancer. As advanced packaging 
and testing capacity is increasingly centralised within leading foundries and mega-
OSATs, tier-1 fabless leaders actively de-risk operations by qualifying secondary 
sources. Ardentec functions as an independent, pure-play testing alternative, providing 
foundry-aligned engineering standards alongside commercial flexibility. This positioning 
allows tier-1 chipmakers to diversify vendor concentration, secure dedicated capacity 
allocations during cyclical tight supply, and mitigate single-point manufacturing 
bottlenecks.

• Technical versatility supported by automotive qualification standards. Ardentec 
maintains a high revenue exposure to automotive and industrial IC testing – sectors that 
demand zero-defect reliability, ultra-wide temperature ranges, and complex mixed-signal 
test protocols. This operational baseline provides significant cross-application versatility. 
During automotive cycle downturns, qualified test cells and probe architectures can be 
reconfigured to support compute, edge AI, and high-frequency communication ICs, 
helping dampen gross margin volatility and stabilising baseline fleet utilisation across 
broader semiconductor cycles.

Ardentec: revenue by product    Ardentec: revenue by application

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts  Source: Company

Longtan: seven-floor mega-fab to scale AI 
Spanning seven vertical cleanroom floors, the company’s Longtan facility began a multi-
year construction timeline starting in 2026, manufacturing semiconductor chips for tier-1 
foundry customers. Management confirmed Floor 1 achieved commercial mass production 
in 3Q26; its +15% MoM revenue inflection in July validated the expansion. At peak 
utilisation, we estimate each fully loaded floor will generate c.TWD150m per month (TWD 
400-500m quarterly) in revenue. With tooling and equipment installations tracking a 
predictable one-floor-per-quarter cadence, each incremental floor can accommodate a 
maximum of 100 high-end automated test equipment (ATE) platforms.

Commanding an hourly billing rate nearly double that of legacy CP testing (95-100% 
premium over legacy CP testing), we estimate each newly commissioned floor will drive 
the company’s top line by 8-10%. We believe this rapid deployment will transform AI/HPC 
sorting from an ancillary segment into Ardentec’s primary growth engine and top-line 
contributor over our forecast horizon.

While Floor 1 capex was self-funded to fast-track qualification, subsequent floor 
expansions are transitioning toward customer-consigned tester models, which will reduce 
balance sheet risk and secure committed wafer allocation. We believe the fleet is custom-
tailored for leading US hyperscaler ASIC roadmaps. By locking in application-specific test 
setups and eliminating idle downtime associated with frequent load-board changeovers, 
Ardentec is able to achieve industry-leading throughput, uncompromised test cell 
efficiency, and maximised asset turns, per management.

Ardentec: capacity expansion    Ardentec: revenue contribution from its Longtan Fab

Proprietary accelerator roadmaps across cloud service providers (CSP) – including AWS 
Trainium/Inferentia, Meta MTIA, and Microsoft Maia – feature starkly heterogeneous probe 
card architectures, thermal docking requirements, and test firmware protocols. We believe 
the modular, multi-story footprint at the company’s Longtan facility provides the physical 
isolation necessary to construct dedicated, IP-secure production cleanrooms for multiple 
hyperscaler customers. As custom AI ASIC testing accounts for a rapidly expanding share 
of the company’s revenue mix relative to legacy automotive exposure, we believe this 
structural diversification serves as a strong catalyst for a rerating for Ardentec towards tier-
1 compute OSAT multiples.

Broad-based UTR inflection on peer capacity spillover 
While automotive and consumer segments navigate cyclical normalisation, Ardentec’s non-
AI capacity is staging a powerful recovery, per management. Based on our channel 
checks, its key OSAT peers (eg, KYEC: 2449 TT, not rated) have aggressively reallocated 
leading-edge test fleets toward mega-cap merchant AI GPUs, creating severe capacity 
bottlenecks for mobile application processor (AP), radio frequency (RF), and networking IC 
designers (such as MediaTek – 2454 TT, TWD4,625, Buy [1], covered by Rick Hsu). 
Therefore, Ardentec is capturing these high-volume, displaced wafer sorting (or chip 
probing, CP) and final test (FT) allocations. This order influx swiftly rebounds mature and 
communication line utilisation rates from trough levels (below 65%) back toward the 80%+ 
threshold, turning legacy capacity into a high-cash-generation baseline with zero asset 
drag.

Semiconductor testing carries a high fixed-cost structure heavily weighted toward 
equipment depreciation and engineering overhead. Historically, once aggregate corporate 
UTR crosses the critical 65% fixed-cost absorption threshold, incremental revenue drops 
directly to the bottom line with minimal variable costs (primarily power and consumables).

Advanced-node ASICs feature extreme sensitivity to thermal dissipation and signal

Next-gen CPO and silicon photonics testing 
Beyond the immediate custom ASIC cycle, Ardentec is securing its next structural growth 
frontier through advanced co-packaged optics (CPO) and silicon photonics (SiPh) wafer-
level testing. As hyperscale AI clusters push copper interconnects to physical transmission 
limits, the industry is transitioning towards optical I/O, which requires testing to shift from 
purely electrical verification to complex electro-optical (E/O) wafer sorting (CP). Unlike 
standard digital logic testing, optical probing demands sub-micron automated fibre 
alignment, precise laser wavelength calibration, optical power loss measurement, and 
specialised thermal-stability testing across photonic integrated circuits (PIC) and electronic 
integrated circuits (EIC) before advanced heterogeneous integration.

transceiver verification. Concurrently, Raytek bolsters the group's specialised optical probe 
station capabilities and precision electro-optical test fixtures.

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
Market Analysis 
Structural shift in the IC testing industry 
Reiterating the key message in our Taiwan Testing Interface Initiation: paradigm shift 
accelerated by AI report, we believe the semiconductor testing paradigm is shifting. In the 
multi-die and 2.5D/3D packaging era (eg, TSMC CoWoS, SoIC), wafer testing (chip 
probing, CP) is no longer a commoditised, post-fab defect screening step – it has evolved 
into a mission-critical gating process. We summarise the critical paradigm shift as follows:

• The known good die (KGD) imperative. Heterogeneous integration bundles high-
value compute dies with multiple high bandwidth memory (HBM) stacks. Packaging an 
untested or marginally defective die risks scrapping the entire multi-thousand-dollar 
subsystem. This zero-tolerance threshold elevates CP from sample-based checks to 
exhaustive, full-spec validation across extreme thermal ranges.

• Structural Test-Time Inflation (2-3x). High reticle-limit AI/HPC custom silicon, 
expanding power envelopes (>700W), and thousands of high-speed interconnect 
channels have doubled or tripled test times per wafer. Consequently, automated test 
equipment (ATE) fleet demand is decoupling from raw silicon unit growth, driven instead 
by compounding tester hours.

• Next-Gen CPO Frontier. Rapid silicon tape-out cadences demand high-density vertical 
probe cards (VPC) and micro-cantilever interfaces supporting parallel multi-site testing. 
Beyond electrical testing, the emergence of CPO and SiPh requires active sub-micron 
optical alignment and electro-optical (E/O) sorting, creating multi-year technical barriers 
and pricing premiums for early movers.

Market dynamics: testing outperforms packaging 
According to Gartner (Semiconductor Assembly and Test Services, Worldwide, Sep 2025, 
Semiconductors and Electronics Team), total package and test market size is projected to 
increase from USD47.3bn to USD65.2bn over 2025-29E, representing an 8.4% CAGR 
over the period. Given that third-party market data typically blends packaging and testing 
into total OSAT, we granularly disaggregate historical revenue across major US and Taiwan 
vendors.

While consumer electronics endured a prolonged post-pandemic inventory correction over 
2022-25, the emergence of AI/HPC workloads created a pronounced divergence across 
the backend: historical industry data indicates that the testing sub-segment delivered 
materially higher year-over-year revenue growth than packaging over 2023-25, propelled 
by multi-die KGD mandates and structural test-time inflation.
